calendar_sync.calendar_link calendar_link_google_calendar_id_check CHECK ((((char_length(google_calendar_id) >= 1) AND (char_length(google_calendar_id) <= 256)) AND (google_calendar_id !~ '[[:cntrl:]]'::text))) validated=true
calendar_sync.calendar_link calendar_link_last_error_code_check CHECK (((last_error_code IS NULL) OR (last_error_code ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_.]{0,63}$'::text))) validated=true

calendar_sync.calendar_link calendar_link_status_check CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['pending_consent'::text, 'active'::text, 'token_revoked'::text, 'error'::text]))) validated=true
calendar_sync.calendar_link calendar_link_sync_token_check CHECK (((sync_token IS NULL) OR ((char_length(sync_token) >= 1) AND (char_length(sync_token) <= 2048)))) validated=true
calendar_sync.calendar_link calendar_link_token_name CHECK ((token_secret_name = ('cal-'::text || (clinician_principal_id)::text))) validated=true

calendar_sync.event_map event_map_google_event_id_check CHECK ((google_event_id ~ '^[a-v0-9]{5,255}$'::text)) validated=true

telehealth.join_token join_token_patient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patient.patient(id) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_principal_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (principal_id) REFERENCES identity.principal(id) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_revoke_reason_check CHECK (((revoke_reason IS NULL) OR (revoke_reason = ANY (ARRAY['superseded'::text, 'session_ended'::text, 'kill_switch'::text, 'vendor_failed'::text, 'removed'::text])))) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_revoked_shape CHECK ((((status = 'revoked'::text) = ((revoked_at IS NOT NULL) AND (revoke_reason IS NOT NULL))) AND ((status = 'revoked'::text) OR ((revoked_at IS NULL) AND (revoke_reason IS NULL))))) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_session_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES telehealth.video_session(id) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_short_lived CHECK (((expires_at > issued_at) AND (expires_at <= (issued_at + '00:15:00'::interval)))) validated=true
telehealth.join_token join_token_status_check CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['issued'::text, 'used'::text, 'revoked'::text]))) validated=true
telehealth.location_confirmation location_confirmation_override CHECK (((override_reason IS NULL) OR (result <> 'allowed'::text))) validated=true
telehealth.location_confirmation location_confirmation_override_reason_check CHECK (((override_reason IS NULL) OR (override_reason = 'licensing_mode_flag'::text))) validated=true

telehealth.location_confirmation location_confirmation_stated_state_check CHECK ((stated_state ~ '^[A-Z]{2}$'::text)) validated=true

telehealth.participant participant_admitted_by_fkey FOREIGN KEY (admitted_by) REFERENCES identity.principal(id) validated=true
telehealth.participant participant_admitted_shape CHECK ((((admission = 'admitted'::text) AND (admitted_at IS NOT NULL)) OR ((admission = 'waiting'::text) AND (admitted_at IS NULL)) OR (admission = 'removed'::text))) validated=true
telehealth.participant participant_device_check_shape CHECK (((device_check IS NULL) OR ((jsonb_typeof(device_check) = 'object'::text) AND (device_check ?& ARRAY['camera'::text, 'mic'::text, 'bandwidth'::text]) AND ((((device_check - 'camera'::text) - 'mic'::text) - 'bandwidth'::text) = '{}'::jsonb) AND ((device_check ->> 'camera'::text) = ANY (ARRAY['pass'::text, 'fail'::text])) AND ((device_check ->> 'mic'::text) = ANY (ARRAY['pass'::text, 'fail'::text])) AND ((device_check ->> 'bandwidth'::text) = ANY (ARRAY['pass'::text, 'fail'::text]))))) validated=true
telehealth.participant participant_device_check_time CHECK (((device_check IS NULL) = (device_checked_at IS NULL))) validated=true

telehealth.video_session video_session_vendor_pair CHECK (((vendor IS NULL) = (vendor_meeting_id IS NULL))) validated=true
